# 1.6.0 — Branch corpus sweep ledger

Half-here-half-there: code that exists on a branch and **not** on trunk.
Trunk pinned at `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172` (`consolidated/1.6.0`).

Six agents append to this file. **Append only. Never rewrite another agent's rows.**

Method (settled): per branch, versus its merge-base with trunk, extract the symbols the
branch ADDS (identifiers, test names, string literals) and search trunk for each — **with
`docs/` and every `*.md` EXCLUDED**, because an audit document describing a gap is prose
about missing code, not the code. `patch-id` is useless (the rename waves moved every path)
and ancestry is useless (3 of 819 branches are ancestors of HEAD). Symbols survive renames;
paths do not. Dedup by `(path, blob)`.

Buckets: **EMPTY** (no source content trunk lacks) · **HARVESTED** (every unique symbol on
trunk, possibly at a renamed path) · **SURVIVOR** (one or more symbols absent from trunk).

Verdict grades used on SURVIVOR rows:
`GAP` real missing work · `SUPERSEDED` trunk implements the same behaviour under another
design/name · `TEST-ONLY` only test names/assertions absent · `PARK` money path, needs a
named owner before anything moves.

---

## Slice: `origin/verify/*` (172 branches) — agent `vk`

`verify/keep-*` names are a **past assertion, not evidence**; every branch in this slice was
treated as unswept. Trunk-side search excludes `docs/` and `*.md` throughout.

### Money path — read at BEHAVIOUR depth, not symbol depth (PARK)

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/verify/keep-fee-unpriced-currency` | SURVIVOR | 47 | 36 | `RateCard::fee_for` · `RateCard::fee_unpriced` · `Unpriceable::FeeUnpriced` · `Divergence::FeeUnpriced` · `Priced.fee_unpriced` (branch: `crates/busbar-unit-cost/src/{rate,posting,project}.rs`, `crates/busbar-unit-ledger/src/recompute.rs`) | **PARK — money.** Branch turns `RateCard::per_request_fee -> i64` into `fee_for -> Option<i64>` so a card that names a currency for its RATES but no FLAT FEE in it fails closed instead of billing 0. Trunk (`crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/src/cost/rate.rs:374`) still has `self.fees.get(&currency).copied().unwrap_or(0)`, and `fee_unit_price_nanos` (`rate.rs:383`) flattens it to `0u128` — a multi-currency card silently bills every flat fee at nothing and reports nothing. Trunk HAS the lane half (`lane_unpriced`, `Unpriceable::LaneUnpriced`, `price_fail_closed`) and is missing only the fee half. Root cause: `unwrap_or(0)` on an absent map key on a billed line. Recommendation: owner decision — port `fee_for -> Option` + `Unpriceable::FeeUnpriced` + `Divergence::FeeUnpriced`, or declare the silent zero intended and say so on the line. Branch also DELETES `derive_spend_cents`/`derive_spend_micros` (USD-pinned 1.5.5 shims) which trunk still carries (17/15 files). |
| `origin/verify/keep-tariff-7` (tip of `tariff-2 ⊂ 3c ⊂ 4 ⊂ 5 ⊂ 5b ⊂ 7`) | SURVIVOR | — | — | `Rounding` (`Bankers`/`Up`/`Down` + `Rounding::divide`) · `PerUnitTerm` · `FlatTerm` · `TariffTerms` · `tier_price` · `metered_dimensions` (branch: `crates/busbar-contract/src/tariff.rs` 332 ln, `crates/busbar-core/src/config/tariff.rs`, `crates/busbar-unit-cost/src/schedule.rs`) | **PARK — money.** The whole tariff/schedule grammar is absent from trunk: `git grep -F tariff` over trunk code (docs excluded) returns **0 files**; 6 doc files mention it. Branch adds declared banker's-rounding (`half to even`, the only unbiased rule) for `amount per N units`, per-plane declared metered dimensions with a **boot refusal** for an amount no unit would pay, and the tier price resolved at one root site and threaded to every leg's journal row. Trunk has `pool_scope` (12 files) so the pool/scope half LANDED; the amounts half did not. Note a latent branch-side defect if ported: `Rounding::Up => whole + 1` is "away from zero" only for non-negative numerators. Recommendation: owner decision on whether 1.6.0 ships priced tariffs at all — this is a feature, not a repair. |
| `origin/verify/keep-ledger-tier` | SURVIVOR (behaviour HARVESTED) | — | — | test names only: `the_reserved_fold_saturates_rather_than_wrapping_at_the_top_of_the_range` · `one_reserved_key_at_the_maximum_is_exact_and_does_not_saturate` · `ordinary_reserved_counts_still_sum_exactly` · `maximal_rate` | **TEST-ONLY — money behaviour already on trunk.** Branch commit `a90ce856d` replaces `acc + (n as u128) * (rate as u128)` with `saturating_mul`/`saturating_add` in `RateNanos::reserved_nanos`. Trunk HAS this: `crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/src/cost/rate.rs:74-83` `nanos_sum` folds with `acc.saturating_add(u128::from(q).saturating_mul(u128::from(r)))`, and both `busbar-kernel/src/cost.rs:199` and `busbar-kernel-budget/src/price.rs:112` delegate to it. Trunk carries its own cells (`cost_tests.rs:795,836,1032`). Nothing to port on the arithmetic; the branch's three cell NAMES are absent but the property they assert is covered. |

## Slice: `origin/delete/*`, rows `NR%3==1` (170 branches) — agent `da`

Derived deterministically:
`for-each-ref refs/remotes/origin | grep '^origin/delete/' | sort | awk 'NR%3==1'` → 170 branches.
Trunk pinned at `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`; re-checked at the end of the sweep.

**Instrument.** Trunk was indexed once into three disjoint tiers — `code`, `docs` (`docs/**` +
every `*.md`) and `qa` (`qa/**`, goldens, waivers, shadow-oracle). "Present on trunk" means
**`code ∪ qa`; `docs` is NEVER counted.** Per branch, the diff against its merge-base yields
definition-site symbols and string literals on ADDED lines; a symbol counts as *branch-authored*
only if it is absent from the merge-base tree (45 distinct merge-bases, each indexed) and is not
also on a removed line — without that subtraction, rename-driven file moves read as thousands of
false additions.

**Bucket distribution (170):** SURVIVOR 142 · EMPTY 17 · HARVESTED 11.
Refined verdicts: BULK-SUPERSEDED 94 · REVIEW 39 · TEST-ONLY 7 · AUDIT-BOOKKEEPING 2.

**Docs-exclusion proof — it changed answers in this slice, repeatedly.**
6,503 identifier tokens exist on trunk in `docs/` but in no code file. Across the slice,
**2,145 symbol/string occurrences on 119 of 170 branches** would have read as "present on trunk"
had `docs/` been searched. Named instances, each verified by hand with
`git grep -w <sym> <TRUNK> -- ':(exclude)docs/'` returning 0 files while the `docs/`-only search
returns ≥1: `CredentialSignature`, `CredentialArrival`, `CredentialPresentation`, `SigningParams`
(whole design, `docs/design/1.6.0-credential-signature-declared.md`, **zero code**);
`RequestHeaders`; `WIRE_HTTP`/`WIRE_STDIO`; `refusal_answer`; `credential_refusal`;
`group_bucket_windows`; `EXPECTED_UNWIRED`; `WiringArm`; `lane_in_pool`; `HostShaped`;
`webpki_client_config`; `KeyRowsRefused`; `CatalogueEntry`; `KeyScope`; `BootFold`.

**Zero control.** Every "absent" verdict was controlled against symbols known present on trunk
using the identical query shape. Index controls: 12 positive (`AccrualMeter`, `AdminAuthChain`,
`interpolate_env`, `reject_yaml_unsafe_value`, `AdminMountSeam`, `splice_occurrences`, …) all
True; 3 negative all False. Shell controls: the same `git grep -c -w … | wc -l` loop that
returned 0 for the survivors returned 21 for `AccrualMeter`, 6 for `AdminAuthChain`, 4 for
`OPENING_CHECKPOINT_SEQ`. Two false-zero traps were hit and fixed during the sweep: zsh parsed
`"$T:crates/…"` as a `:c` history modifier (fixed to `${T}:`), and an early run tested
branch-side `qa/` manifest content against a **code-only** trunk index, manufacturing false
survivors (3 of 4 sampled auditor ids were in fact on trunk in `qa/audit-ledger.json`) — fixed by
making presence `code ∪ qa`, then re-running all 170.

### Money path — read at BEHAVIOUR depth (PARK)

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/delete/keep-meter-gap` | SURVIVOR | 78 | 42 | `UNITS_IMPL_SIGNATURE` · `units_impl_meter_step` · `has_units_impl` · `EXPECTED_UNWIRED` · `WiringArm` · `wiring_arm` · `selftest_a_free_fn_meter_is_not_the_loops_meter_step` · `selftest_expected_unwired_names_the_gap_and_only_the_gap` · `lane_in_pool` · `HostShaped` (branch: `crates/busbar/tests/plane_meter_seam_reachability.rs`, `crates/busbar/src/root/units_mcp.rs`) | **PARK — money.** The billing-reachability gate on trunk locates a plane's Teller **Meter step** as `common::item_body(&lines, "fn meter(")` over the WHOLE leg file (`crates/busbar/tests/plane_meter_seam_reachability.rs:220`). A **free** `fn meter(...)` that is on no `impl … Units for …` block — therefore callable by nobody, since the Teller loop holds the leg and calls the method on it — satisfies that gate while the leg contributes **no Meter step at all**, i.e. nothing it serves over the root is ever priced. The branch replaces the file-wide search with `units_impl_meter_step()`: scope to the `impl … Units for …` block, then require a `&self`/`&mut self` receiver; plus `EXPECTED_UNWIRED` to name known-unwired legs and two selftests that prove the gate would go red on a free `fn meter`. Root cause: the gate asks the FILE a question it must ask the TRAIT METHOD. Recommendation: owner decision — forward-port `units_impl_meter_step` + the two selftests (the branch predates the `busbar_unit_usage::meter(` → `busbar_kernel_ledger::usage::meter(` rename, so this is a port, not a cherry-pick). **Same hardening independently present on `origin/delete/keep-root-mcp-fix` (513 ln vs trunk's 387) — two branches carry it, trunk carries neither.** |
| `origin/delete/keep-root-mcp-fix` | SURVIVOR | 63 | 42 | `UNITS_IMPL_SIGNATURE` · `units_impl_meter_step` (branch: `crates/busbar/tests/plane_meter_seam_reachability.rs`) | **PARK — money (duplicate of the row above).** Second, independent copy of the Meter-step-location hardening. Its own prose names the defect: *"THE THIRD FAILURE. The step used to be located by the first `fn meter(` ANYWHERE in the leg file, so a FREE function nothing calls satisfied a gate asking whether the loop's Meter step meters."* Corroborates the finding; port once, from whichever branch is the fuller text (`keep-meter-gap`). |
| `origin/delete/keep-m9-migration` | SURVIVOR | 121 | 119 | `OPENING_HISTORY_SEQ` · `the_opening_entries_carry_the_opening_history_entry` (branch: `crates/busbar-unit-ledger/src/migration.rs`) | **PARK — money.** Trunk's ledger migration still takes the rate card as a **caller-supplied parameter**: `migrate(…, rate_card_version: u64, …)` (`crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/src/migration.rs:428`) → `opening_balances(&head, rate_card_version)` (`legacy.rs:173`), and the root hands it an operator config value verbatim — `migrate(rows, records, cfg.node, wall, cfg.rate_card_version, secret)` (`crates/busbar/src/root/migration.rs:203`). Nothing validates it, so a year of opening balances can be sealed priced under an arbitrary card. The branch replaces the parameter with `pub const OPENING_HISTORY_SEQ: u64 = 0` — *"a parameter is a place a mistake can be made, and there is exactly one entry a migration may open at"* — keeping the field at its offset so the marker's 88 bytes do not move. Trunk already accepts this pattern next door (`OPENING_CHECKPOINT_SEQ`, 4 files), which is corroborating rather than decisive. Root cause: operator-supplied value on a billed line with no validation. Recommendation: owner decision — pin to the sealed history entry, or state on the line why an operator may choose it. |
| `origin/delete/keep-a2a-terminal-seam` | SURVIVOR | 79 | 32 | `UNDER_REPORTED` · `a_provider_usage_shortfall_reaches_the_money_path` · `the_shortfall_is_never_folded_into_a_class_it_was_not_consumed_under` (branch: `crates/busbar-plane-llm/tests/usage_identity.rs`) | **SUPERSEDED — money, resolved differently and better.** Branch carries a deliberately-`#[ignore]`d red-by-design test asserting a Gemini provider under-report (stated `usageMetadata.totalTokenCount` > sum of decoded buckets) must REACH the money path rather than being computed, threaded and dropped. Trunk resolved the underlying defect instead of keeping the red: `crates/busbar-llm-codec/src/gemini/mod.rs:354-369` attributes the shortfall, and `gemini/tests/usage_identity_tests.rs:150,232` assert *"the shortfall IS the tool-use term"* and *"every term Google stated is now billed, so there is no shortfall left to report."* Nothing to port. Recorded because the branch's test NAME is the only in-code record of the original decision. |
| `origin/delete/keep-root-wiring` | SURVIVOR | 176 | 141 | `group_bucket_windows` · `KeyRowsRefused` · `webpki_client_config` · `boot_checks` · `a_store_that_will_not_list_its_key_rows_still_opens_the_configured_buckets` · `the_boot_step_seals_the_opening_on_the_nodes_one_book` (branch: `crates/busbar-core/src/cost.rs`, `crates/busbar/src/root/migration.rs`) | **REVIEW — money-adjacent boot/seal path.** `group_bucket_windows` (cost grouping) and the migration boot-seal cells (`KeyRowsRefused`, *"a store that will not list its key rows still opens the configured buckets"*) are absent from trunk code; `group_bucket_windows` and `KeyRowsRefused` each survive on trunk **only in `docs/`**. Branch is off an old base (`crates/busbar-core/` is split on trunk), so paths moved — but the trunk-side symbol search is path-independent and returns 0. Recommendation: owner read of whether trunk's post-split cost/migration code covers the same two behaviours under other names; not certified either way here. |

### Auth / credential path

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/delete/keep-auth-e` | SURVIVOR | 231 | 136 | `CredentialSignature` · `CredentialArrival` · `CredentialPresentation` · `read_arrival` · `SigningParams` · `AT_AUTHORIZATION_BEARER` · `AS_AUTHORIZATION_BEARER` · `NO_SIGNING` · …(+87) (branch: `crates/busbar-contract/src/kinds.rs`, `crates/busbar-unit-auth/src/carrier.rs`, `crates/busbar-unit-egress-auth/src/lib.rs`) | **GAP — documented-as-designed, code never landed.** The declared-credential-signature grammar (a credential's carriers DECLARED per dialect — worded vs verbatim carriers, scheme-word matched case-insensitively, empty falls through, an unmounted dialect refused by the declaration's absence) exists on trunk **only as `docs/design/1.6.0-credential-signature-declared.md`**. `git grep -w CredentialSignature <TRUNK> -- ':(exclude)docs/'` → **0 files**; same for `CredentialArrival`, `CredentialPresentation`, `SigningParams`. This is the exact trap the sweep was warned about, at full scale: the design doc reads as the feature. Branch carries the implementation plus 11 carrier cells. Recommendation: owner decision — port, or retract the doc so it stops reading as landed. |
| `origin/delete/keep-r5-auth-2` | SURVIVOR | 44 | 43 | `RequestHeaders` (branch: `crates/busbar-core/src/auth/mod.rs`) | **GAP — two live copies of one credential-precedence rule.** Trunk carries the carrier unit at `crates/busbar-kernel-identity/src/carrier.rs` (`extract_client_token`, `HeaderView`, `X_API_KEY`, `X_GOOG_API_KEY`, `AUTH_SCHEME_BEARER`) — but its **only `HeaderView` impl is in `crates/busbar-kernel-identity/src/tests/mod.rs:118`**, so the unit is wired to nothing in production. The live path is a **second, independent copy**: `AuthMiddleware::extract_client_token(&Request<Body>)` at `crates/busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs:669` with its own duplicate constants (lines 27/28/33), called from `auth/exchange.rs:55` and `auth/mod.rs:1474`. The two agree today; nothing keeps them agreeing. The branch's `RequestHeaders` newtype is precisely the missing adapter that collapses them to one (branch commit: *"ONE copy of the credential carriers"*). Root cause: a de-dup that landed the unit but never switched the caller. Recommendation: port the adapter and delete the kernel-local copy, or the precedence rule (Bearer → `x-api-key` → `x-goog-api-key`, empty-is-absent, non-Bearer falls through) must be kept in lockstep in two places by hand. |
| `origin/delete/keep-admin-c1` | SURVIVOR | 46 | 43 | `refusal_answer` · `credential_refusal` · `without_admin_carriers` (branch: `crates/busbar/src/root/units_admin.rs`) | **REVIEW — admin credential refusal.** Three refusal-path symbols absent from trunk code; `refusal_answer` and `credential_refusal` survive on trunk **only in `docs/`**. Branch composes an admin refusal answer for a request presenting no admin carrier. Recommendation: owner read of whether trunk's post-split admin auth expresses the same refusal under another name. |
| `origin/delete/keep-stepown-sos` | SURVIVOR | 96 | 94 | `WIRE_HTTP` · `WIRE_STDIO` (branch: `crates/busbar-substrate-values/src/lib.rs`) | **REVIEW — egress wire-kind constants.** Both absent from trunk code, both present on trunk in `docs/` only. Branch centralises the wire-kind spellings used by `net_guard.rs` and the two `egress_auth` modules so the string is stated once. Low blast radius; likely superseded by a different constant name on trunk, but the two spellings themselves return 0. |

---

## Slice: `origin/delete/*`, rows `NR%3==0` (169 branches) — agent `dc`

Slice derived deterministically and disjointly:
`for-each-ref refs/remotes/origin | grep '^origin/delete/' | sort | awk 'NR%3==0'` → 169 branches,
169 distinct tip trees (no duplicate-tree branches in this slice).
Trunk pinned at `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`; re-checked unmoved at the end of the sweep.

### Instrument

The pinned tree was extracted to a scratch dir (never a checkout of the shared worktree) and every
identifier in it tokenised with `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*` into three disjoint sets — **code**
(`*.rs|sh|py|js|mjs|ts` outside `docs/`), **docs** (everything under `docs/`), and **other**
(goldens, `*.json`, `*.toml`, workflows). Buckets are decided against the **code** set alone.
Per branch, the symbols ADDED between `merge-base(trunk, branch)` and the branch tip are extracted
from the added lines of code files only, and looked up in that set.

### Docs-exclusion proof — it changed answers in this slice

Not a silence: **6 branches flip `HARVESTED` → `SURVIVOR` when `docs/` is excluded**, and across the
slice **2124 absent-symbol hits on 129 branches** resolve on trunk *only* inside `docs/` prose.

| branch | naive bucket (docs included) | correct bucket (docs excluded) | symbols that exist on trunk only as prose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/delete/keep-kind-isolation-gate` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `MEASURED_EDGES`, `UNSCORED_SOURCES`, `render_graph` |
| `origin/delete/keep-xtask-findings` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `SPEC_SINK`, `MEASURED_EDGES`, `UNSCORED_SOURCES`, `render_graph` |
| `origin/delete/keep-one-face-home-per-kind` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `HttpEndpointRequest`, `HttpEndpointResponse`, `one_face_home` |
| `origin/delete/keep-red-portstest` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `groups_chain` |
| `origin/delete/keep-tests-p1` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `every_known_protocol_reads_an_empty_conversation_to_an_empty_ir` |
| `origin/delete/keep-xtask-batch1c` | HARVESTED | **SURVIVOR** | `list_all_and_a_named_gate_are_green_over_the_real_tree` |

The cleanest single instance: `keep-tariff`'s `DisputePolicy` and `TariffCfg` exist on trunk in
exactly two files — `docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md` and `docs/design/1.6.0-local-only-audit.md` — and in
**zero** `.rs` files. Both are audit documents *describing the gap*. A trunk-side search that reads
`docs/` calls the whole tariff section harvested on the strength of the note saying it is missing.

**Caveat found while proving it, and it cuts the other way:** a docs mention is not always evidence of
a gap. `docs/design/xtask-gates.md:1422` says of two of the symbols above that *"`UNSCORED_SOURCES`
and `SPEC_SINK` are gone"* — a deliberate retirement, not a loss. Docs must be excluded from the
SEARCH and then read for INTENT on whatever the search flags.

### Calibration — where this method over-reports, measured

The premise "symbols survive renames" is only **partly** true in this repo, and that is the dominant
error term in the raw 144-survivor count:

- **Test names are re-worded when work lands.** Proved on `keep-rate-window-sweep-enforcing`: the
  branch's cell `clock_regression_does_not_reset_the_spent_budget` is absent from trunk by name, yet
  trunk proves the same property at `crates/busbar-core-admin/src/tests/rate_tests.rs:303` as
  `a_regressing_clock_is_clamped_into_the_live_window_rather_than_opening_an_older_one`. All
  13 `TEST-ONLY` rows below therefore carry **low confidence** and need a property-level read.
- **Production type names were re-spelled too.** The `busbar-contract-transport` session seam
  (`SessionDriver`, `DuplexWire`, `SessionBudgets`) is absent by name, but trunk carries
  `SessionPump` / `SessionLease` (`crates/busbar/src/root/units_voice.rs:316,328`), `SessionView`
  (`crates/busbar-contract/src/unit.rs:442`) and `DuplexReader`/`DuplexWriter`
  (`crates/busbar-voice-codec/src/ir/codec/mod.rs:536,561`); 103 trunk `.rs` files mention duplex.
  Hence the `ADJUDICATE` grade: absent by name, concept present, behaviour unread.

So the rows are graded, not just bucketed: **`GAP (verified)`** the concept is absent from trunk code
outright · **`PARK (money)`** money path, owner required · **`ADJUDICATE`** absent by name only,
needs a behaviour read · **`TEST-ONLY (low confidence)`** · **`NO GAP`**.

Grade distribution over the 169: `ADJUDICATE` 77 · `PARK (money)` 39 · `NO GAP` 29 ·
`TEST-ONLY (low confidence)` 13 · `GAP (verified)` 10 · `GAP (low severity)` 1.

### Second instrument — cross-check against trunk's own prior audit

Trunk already carries per-branch verdicts for all 169 of these branches in
`docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md` and `docs/design/1.6.0-local-only-audit.md`. Parsed and compared
(this is reading docs for *intent*, not searching them for symbols): **145/169 agree, 24 disagree.**
Twelve of the 24 are `EMPTY` vs `HARVESTED` — both mean "nothing to take". The material ones are
resolved inline in the rows; the three worth naming here:

- `keep-rate-window-sweep-enforcing` — **I was wrong, the prior audit was right** (test-name rename).
- `keep-plane-llm-stack`, `keep-oracle-fix`, `keep-oracle-corpus-p3` — **I was wrong**: `.audit/`
  scratch files and helpers that now live in the extracted `GetBusbar/busbar-oracle` repo.
- `keep-stepown-som` — **the prior audit was wrong**, graded HARVESTED at symbol depth. It is a
  behaviour/hygiene delta that adds no symbol, so no symbol method can see it. Details in the row.

### What this method still cannot see

Behaviour changes that add no new symbol. The three "EMPTY" branches with real code deltas were
therefore hand-read (`keep-stepown-som`, `keep-r5-auth`, `keep-llm-spec-conformance`), as were every
`EMPTY`/`HARVESTED` branch whose diff contained arithmetic or comparison operators, and the money
path throughout. One finding came out of it that no symbol pass could have produced
(`keep-stepown-som`).

### Zeros controlled

Every "absent" answer was taken twice, by two independent instruments that agree: exact-token set
membership in Python, and `grep` over the extracted tree. Positive control `saturating_add`
(306 `.rs` lines / 109 files) and negative control `zzq_nonexistent_symbol_xyz` (0) were asserted
before each pass. Two live false-zero traps were hit and fixed during the sweep: **zsh glob-expanded
an unquoted `--include=*.rs`**, silently returning "no matches found" for symbols that were present;
and `grep -c` was replaced with line counts where occurrence counts were meant. A third was
*suspected and disproved* — `\btotal_of\b` returning 0 while plain `total_of` returned 3 looked like
the POSIX-ERE `\b` trap, but a direct probe showed the boundary works and the 3 hits were
`total_offenders` substrings, so `total_of` really is absent.

| `origin/delete/keep-substrate-fix` | `zeroize_key` | **SUPERSEDED, and trunk's version is stronger.** Trunk carries the identical body — same `Arc::get_mut` sole-owner guard, same `PrivateKeyDer` `Zeroize`-not-`ZeroizeOnDrop` reasoning — renamed to `wipe()` at `crates/busbar-kernel/src/egress/engine/tls.rs:116`, and unlike the branch it is called from `Drop` and kept a separate function so the wipe is observable to a test. ## Slice `origin/verify/*` — agent `vk`, batch 2

Grades aligned to agent `dc`'s vocabulary (`GAP (verified)` · `PARK (money)` · `ADJUDICATE` ·
`TEST-ONLY (low confidence)` · `NO GAP`), so the owner gets one list and not six.

### Security — `GAP (verified)`, hand-read to the line

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/verify/keep-tests-plugins` | SURVIVOR | 19 test fns added | 13 | `the_ref_resolver_caps_its_own_depth` · `a_malformed_sign_key_is_reported_without_echoing_it` · `the_ref_resolver`'s `depth: u32` / `MAX_RESOLVE_DEPTH` param (branch: `crates/plugin-pack/src/main.rs:240-253`, `crates/plugin-pack/src/tests/main_tests.rs:912`) | **GAP (verified) — unbounded recursion on operator-supplied input.** Trunk `crates/plugin-sdk/src/pack.rs:218` `resolve_effective(schema, defs, resolving)` takes **no depth argument and has no cap**; its only guard is the cycle set `resolving: &mut HashSet<String>`. A NON-cyclic `allOf`/`$ref` chain (`L0 -> L1 -> … -> L200`) therefore recurses once per link — nothing repeats, so the cycle guard never fires, and `scan`'s `MAX_SCAN_DEPTH = 64` (`pack.rs:321`) never advances inside a resolve chain. Stack overflow, abort, no refusal. The branch carries **both halves of the repair**: `resolve_effective(..., depth: u32)` with `const MAX_RESOLVE_DEPTH: u32 = 64` returning `"…nests deeper than 64 levels while resolving $ref/allOf…"`, and the red cell that distinguishes the resolver's cap from `scan`'s (it asserts the message contains `while resolving`, precisely so a refusal from the other counter cannot be mistaken for this one). Same defect the trunk LEDGER already carries as **BR-S2**; this row adds the branch that already contains the fix and its proof. |

### Money path — `PARK`, read at BEHAVIOUR depth

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/verify/keep-mount-chain-funded-6` (tip of `funded-2 ⊂ funded-5 ⊂ funded-6`) | SURVIVOR | — | — | `Ledger::post_lent` · `Ledger::book_posting` · `Booked` · `PostingLent` · `UnitEnd::lend_posting` (branch: `crates/busbar-unit-ledger/src/{settle,rows,lib}.rs`) | **PARK — money.** Adds a THIRD settlement door for the one caller that can neither hand over a hold nor give up the posting: a plane behind a mount owes its mount the kernel's sealed end, and settling through `Ledger::post` (which takes `Posted` **by value**) destroys it. The branch factors the arithmetic into a private `book_posting(&Posted)` both doors share — so a lent settlement and an owned one cannot post differently — and makes the lent door take a `PostingLent<'_>` witness rather than a `&Posted`, because a bare reference would make the door drivable from any borrow and the exactly-once property would have nothing holding it up. Trunk has **`Ledger::post` only** (`crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/src/settle.rs:176`); `PostingLent`, `post_lent`, `lend_posting`, `book_posting`, `Booked` are **0 code files and 0 doc files** on trunk. Recommendation: owner decides whether trunk's mount path needs to settle from a borrow; if it does, this is the exactly-once shape to take. |
| `origin/verify/keep-kernel-fee-identity` (tip of `kernel-contract-arena ⊂ kernel-status-leg ⊂ kernel-fee-identity`) | SURVIVOR | — | — | `fee_identity` · `settled_fee` · the `head: Option<&StatusLeg>` parameter on `teller::fee_count` | **PARK — money, but the decision TABLE is byte-identical.** Branch `teller::fee_count(evidence, head)` and trunk `teller::fee_count(evidence)` (`crates/busbar-kernel/src/teller.rs:440`) have the **same eight match arms in the same order**. The delta is the SOURCE: the branch reads `head.delivered` / `head.status` / `head.finish` / `head.at` off one `StatusLeg` the unit carries, where trunk reads `evidence.relayed_first_response_frame` / `evidence.status` / `evidence.finish` / `evidence.status_at` off `FeeEvidence`. `FeeEvidence` (6 files) and `StatusLeg` (1 file) both exist on trunk; `fee_identity` and `settled_fee` are **docs-only**. The branch's claim is that trunk's two sources can disagree and the branch's one cannot. ### `vk` batch 3 — METHOD CORRECTION applied, and what it moved

The coordinator's correction (absent-by-name ≠ gap; read docs for INTENT; cross-check the prior
audit; hand-read EMPTY branches with a live code delta) was applied to every row above. An
adjudication instrument was built for it: trunk's code identifiers are split on `CamelCase`/`snake`
into words, indexed word→identifier with IDF weighting, so for any absent symbol the question
*"what does trunk have that looks like this"* is answered with evidence instead of a guess. Its
controls: `SessionDriver` → `UnitDriver`; `DuplexWire` → `DuplexWriter`/`DuplexSession`/
`IrDuplexControl` — i.e. it independently reproduces the two renames the finished slice used to
falsify the method, which is why its silences are worth something.

**Movements from the correction (4 rows re-graded, 1 upgraded):**

| row | was | now | why |
|---|---|---|---|
| `keep-fee-unpriced-currency` | PARK (symbol depth) | **PARK (money) — GAP VERIFIED at behaviour depth** | the reachability was traced through trunk, not inferred; see below |
| `keep-mount-chain-funded-6` | PARK | **PARK (money) — ADJUDICATE on need, GAP on code** | adjudicator found `lent` on trunk; it is the caps-token prose, not a ledger door |
| `keep-arena-*` (4) | SURVIVOR | **NO GAP (confirmed twice)** | adjudicator pointed at trunk `ARENA`; hand-read shows all five hits are PROSE in comments plus `PlaneAllocBudget`, not a type. `Scratch` remains the replacement and trunk's own docs call the branch design retired |
| `keep-ledger-tier`, `keep-tests-money-units`, `keep-fee-status-leg-all-planes` | TEST-ONLY | **NO GAP on behaviour / TEST-ONLY (low confidence) on names** | unchanged in substance, restated in the shared vocabulary |
| `keep-tariff-*` (6) | PARK | **PARK (money) — and now corroborated by a second instrument** | see cross-check below |

**`keep-fee-unpriced-currency` — the reachability, traced on trunk, line by line.** This is no
longer "the symbol is absent". The silent under-bill is *reachable* on trunk:

1. `RateCard::set_rate` (`crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/src/cost/rate.rs:332`) inserts the currency
   into `self.currencies` **and `self.prices`** — and **not** into `self.fees`.
2. `RateCard::set_fee` (`rate.rs:344`) is the only writer of `self.fees`. Nothing obliges a caller
   to call both, so a card whose second currency was priced by hand has
   `currencies ∋ C`, `prices ∋ C`, **`fees ∌ C`**.
3. `prices_currency(C)` (`rate.rs:364`) tests `self.currencies` → **true**, so
   `MoneyError::CurrencyNotPriced` does not fire (`cost/view.rs:369`).
4. `lane_rates(lane, C)` answers `Some`, so `MoneyError::LaneUnpriced` does not fire
   (`view.rs:374`).
5. `fee_term` (`view.rs:429-434`) then reads `card.per_request_fee(C)` →
   `self.fees.get(&C).copied().unwrap_or(0)` (`rate.rs:374`) → **0**.

Every refusal on the path is bypassed and the flat fee bills **zero, silently, on every request in
that currency**, through `price_ledger` — the ONE function. `fee_unit_price_nanos` (`rate.rs:383`)
flattens the same absence to `0u128` on the posting path, and trunk's cell
`rate_tests.rs:96` *asserts* the zero. Trunk already knows the LANE half of this shape and has a
cell for it — `crates/busbar/tests/money_one_function_equivalence.rs:434`
`d4_an_unpriced_lane_bills_as_free_on_every_read_and_refuses_only_in_the_one_function` — and the
FEE half is the untested sibling. **Cell:** `rate.rs:374` / `view.rs:434`. **Root cause:**
`unwrap_or(0)` on an absent map key on a billed line, where the adjacent lane case refuses.
**Recommendation (owner, money is binary):** port the branch's `fee_for -> Option<i64>` +
`RateCard::fee_unpriced` + `Unpriceable::FeeUnpriced` + `Divergence::FeeUnpriced`, **or** make
`set_rate` insert a fee so the state is unreachable, **or** record the zero as intended on the line.
Not self-approved either way.

### Second instrument — cross-check against trunk's own prior audit

`docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md` carries a prior per-branch verdict for **171 of my 172** (agents `ag`
and `ah`). `docs/design/1.6.0-local-only-audit.md` is organised by subsystem and names none of them.

- **Bucket agreement: 171/171.** Every prior row says `SURVIVOR`; so does my symbol pass.
- **But 164 of those 171 are stamped `depth SYMBOL-ONLY — behaviour NOT verified`** and 7
  `ADJUDICATED`. So the agreement is agreement about a *question*, not an answer — which is exactly
  the correction's point. A 172/172 SURVIVOR count has no discriminating power and should not be
  reported as 172 gaps.
- **One branch the prior audit MISSES entirely: `origin/verify/keep-queue-AB2`** — no row in
  `1.6.0-LEDGER.md`. Swept here; row below.
- **Agreement on the biggest finding, reached independently:** `BR-M1` (`1.6.0-LEDGER.md:943`)
  concludes the tariff/pricing subsystem is absent from trunk in its entirety, citing the *same*
  evidence this sweep produced without seeing it — `enum Rounding` → 0 files, `Bankers` → 0,
  `half to even` → 0 — and the same reasoning about half-to-even being the only unbiased rule. It
  adds something this sweep did not have: **`docs/migration-1.6.md:97` ships the user-facing claim
  that a configured `session_fee` "charges once per session, exactly as configured" for code that
  is not in the tree.** Status there is `PARKED-OWNER`; concur.
- **Docs read for INTENT, per the correction:** trunk's `crates/busbar-contract/src/scratch.rs`
  header states the fixed-cap arena is *"the dead fixed-cap arena"* and `PlaneAlloc` *"the retired
  `PlaneAlloc` trait"* that DECISIONS #41 replaces. So the four `keep-*arena*` branches are a
  **deliberate retirement, not a loss** — their refusal-on-full semantics are the thing #41 removed,
  and porting them would reintroduce it. A `keep-` name is not evidence; this is the case that
  proves it in my slice.

## Slice: `origin/land/*` (44) + unprefixed `origin/<name>` (43) — agent `ln`

Pinned at `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`; **every finding below re-verified at
`6ddd7975c198a21dfdae83b273ec680ad664c702`** after trunk moved mid-sweep (5 commits landed —
4 sweep/audit/docs, 1 config; none touched a finding). 87 branches, all resolved
(`refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` alias `origin` excluded, leaving 44 + 43).

**Topology matters for reading these rows.** All 44 `land/*` branches fan out from ONE base,
`dc7bb3233bf9cf73f02b7d0afd59cbce05d022b6`, and each is 150–162 commits ahead of it while the
whole set holds only **222 distinct non-merge commits** — they carry each other's work by
cherry-pick. So the 44 branches' absent-symbol sets overlap massively: they collapse to **389
unique absent symbols in 139 branch-side file clusters**, and the finding is the CLUSTER, not
the branch. A cluster appearing on 41 branches was cherry-picked 41 times and landed zero.

### NAT64 — the loss that named this sweep is REPAIRED

`64:ff9b` is on trunk in 5 code files. `crates/busbar-kernel-egress/src/trust/net.rs`
`embedded_ipv4()` unwraps BOTH RFC 6052 `64:ff9b::/96` (well-known) AND RFC 8215
`64:ff9b:1::/48` at any `/96` instantiation including a non-zero-padded middle (RFC 8215 §6's
own `64:ff9b:1:fffe::/96` example), before any IMDS/link-local/private judgement. Cells in
`busbar-kernel/src/tests/net_guard_tests.rs` and `busbar-mcp/.../argguard_tests.rs`. Both the
S2/S27 half and the D27/D28 local-use half landed. `land/net-guard` and
`land/held-netguard-dedup` are HARVESTED on this point.

### SECURITY — real gaps, live on trunk

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/land/secret-leak` (S6-rest(b)) | SURVIVOR | 76 | 45 | `sign_key_hex_error` · `sign_key_hex_error_never_echoes_the_offending_value` (branch: `crates/plugin-pack/src/main.rs`, `.../tests/main_tests.rs`) | **GAP — security, secret in a log line.** Trunk `crates/plugin-sdk/src/pack.rs:538` (the renamed `plugin-pack`) still does `hex::decode(hex_seed.trim()).map_err(\|e\| format!("{SIGN_KEY_ENV} is not valid hex: {e}"))`. `hex-0.4.3`'s `FromHexError::InvalidHexCharacter{c,index}` Display writes the offending **character and its byte index** — so a malformed `$BUSBAR_SIGN_KEY` (the 32-byte ed25519 signing seed) prints a character of the seed value and its position into a packaging error, which is exactly what lands in a CI log or a pasted bug report. Branch replaces the whole `{e}` with a fixed `sign_key_hex_error()` naming only the variable and the required shape. Root cause: interpolating a decoder error over secret bytes. Recommendation: port the one-line `.map_err(\|_\| sign_key_hex_error())` + its cell. Note trunk ships `scripts/secret-hygiene-gate.sh`, which did not catch this. |
| `origin/land/busbar-mcp` (D17) — also on 9 other `land/*` | SURVIVOR | 305 | 137 | `MAX_SAMPLING_MESSAGES` · `MAX_SAMPLING_PROMPT_BYTES` · `MAX_STOP_SEQUENCES` · `MAX_STOP_SEQUENCE_BYTES` · `oversized_prompt` · `text_message` · `an_ask_carrying_more_messages_than_the_cap_is_refused_before_any_body_is_built` · `an_ask_whose_prompt_exceeds_the_byte_bound_is_refused_however_it_is_split` · `a_temperature_outside_the_protocols_range_is_refused_rather_than_forwarded` · `stop_sequences_are_counted_measured_and_typed` (branch: `crates/busbar-mcp/src/mcp/sampling.rs`, `.../tests/sampling_spend_tests.rs`) | **GAP — security + caller's money.** An UPSTREAM MCP server's `sampling/createMessage` ask is completed on the CALLER's budget. Trunk's `chat_body` (`crates/busbar-mcp/src/mcp/sampling.rs`) accepts an **unbounded** message count and unbounded total prompt bytes, and forwards `temperature` (any JSON number) and `stopSequences` (any array, any length, any element size) **verbatim** to the provider. Trunk's `SamplingSpend` bounds how MANY completions an upstream induces per minute; nothing bounds how LARGE one is — the branch's own words: *"the prompt an upstream sends is spent against the CALLER'S budget, so its size is bounded here rather than by the caller who never wrote it."* Branch adds 64 messages / 64 KiB total prompt / 8 stop sequences / 64 B each, refused before any body is built. `MAX_COMPLETION_BYTES` (8 MiB, on trunk) bounds the completion, not the ask. Recommendation: port the four consts and their four refusal sites. The D17 second half — *log* busbar's own pipeline refusal instead of relaying it — is **deliberately divergent on trunk** (`sampling.rs:294-295` relays it on purpose, documented); do not port that half without an owner call. |

Scope note for the owner: this crate is the in-tree ABI-crossing store EXAMPLE and the store-conformance subject — it is the reference an operator copies, and it is what the conformance battery measures, so a wrong answer here propagates. ### The docs-exclusion proof, for this slice

**104 of the 389 unique absent symbols across the 44 `land/*` branches (26.7%) are present on
trunk ONLY in `docs/` or `*.md`.** Without the exclusion each of those 104 would have read as
"present on trunk" and the branch would have been mis-bucketed HARVESTED. The exclusion did not
merely change "at least one" answer — it changed **more than a quarter of them**, and it changed
the answer on the highest-value rows in this slice:

- `kind_abi_lane` — 0 code files, **3 doc files** (the #30 gate, cherry-picked 41 times, landed 0)
- `WIRE_PROTOCOL_WORDS` — 0 code, **2 docs**
- `MeterEvent` — 0 code, **1 doc** (the money-model uniform metering ABI)
- `MeteringEntry` — 0 code, **2 docs**; `UsageRow` — 0 code, **1 doc** (the `FileStore` money PARK)
- `MAX_SAMPLING_MESSAGES` / `MAX_SAMPLING_PROMPT_BYTES` / `MAX_STOP_SEQUENCES` — 0 code, **2 docs each**
- `MAX_RETAINED_GENERATIONS` — 0 code, **2 docs** (S28)
- `sign_key_hex_error` — 0 code, **2 docs** (the live signing-seed leak)
- `KeyScope` — 0 code, **2 docs**; `ReservedPort` — 0 code, **1 doc**; `WALK_DEPTH_CAP` — 0 code, **1 doc**

The single worst offender is `docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md` (549 KB of prose describing exactly
these gaps). An audit document that says "X is missing" contains the token `X`, and a trunk-side
search that reads `docs/` reports `X` as present. That is the 33% false-negative mechanism,
reproduced and measured here at 26.7%.

### How the zeros were controlled

Every zero in this slice was produced by **two independent instruments that had to agree**: a
pickled identifier index built by walking `git ls-tree -r` + `git cat-file --batch` over all 3630
non-doc trunk blobs in Python (exact set membership, no shell, no regex boundary), and
`git grep -l -F <literal> <sha> -- ':(exclude)docs/' ':(exclude)*.md'` at the NEW trunk tip. All
14 headline symbols were re-checked through the second instrument after trunk moved; all agreed.

Traps that fired during this sweep and were caught:
- **zsh does not word-split an unquoted `$var`.** `git merge-base --octopus $LB` passed all 44
  refs as ONE newline-containing argument; git's error echoed the whole blob, which reads at a
  glance like a per-ref failure list. Replaced with `set --`/`while IFS= read -r`.
- **`"$T:crates/..."` parses `:c` as a zsh modifier.** `git show $T:crates/...` silently became
  `d2548391...rates/...` and returned "unknown revision". Every such read uses `"${T}:path"`.
- **`grep -c` counts lines, not occurrences**, and `git grep -c` prints nothing on no-match — so
  an `rc` captured after a pipe is `head`'s, not git's. No zero in this report rests on a piped rc.
- **A suspiciously round result was treated as an instrument fault until disproved.**
  `vt13-owning-held` reported exactly `1000` symbols / `500` absent / `100` docs-only. Three exact
  round numbers is not coincidence, so the branch was recounted with a second, independently
  written regex: `1005` defs / `506` absent / `100` docs-only. The magnitude is real; the round
  figures came from the main tool's stop-word filter and string-literal inclusion. Recorded rather
  than quietly accepted.
- **`patch-id` and ancestry were not used at all**, per the settled method.

### What this method cannot see, stated plainly

A symbol diff is blind to a behaviour change that adds no new symbol. Every `land/*` row above
that says GAP or SUPERSEDED was read at DIFF depth, not symbol depth — which is how the
`PushLimiter` backward-clock clamp (trunk has it, branch does not), the `read_count_u64` 2^53
bound (trunk stricter than the branch), the `FileStore` nine-method `self.inner` delegation
(no missing symbol at all — the methods are all PRESENT, they just delegate to RAM), and the
trunk `secret` loader's exact-match ABI window were found. None of those four would have
appeared in a symbol bucket. The four `r*` branches and `qa` are explicitly marked LOW
CONFIDENCE because they did NOT get that read.

## Slice: `origin/delete/*`, rows `NR%3==2` (169 branches) — agent `db`

Slice derived deterministically:

```sh
git for-each-ref --format='%(refname:short)' refs/remotes/origin \
  | grep '^origin/delete/' | sort | awk 'NR%3==2'   # 169 of 508
```

Trunk pinned at `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`; re-checked at the end of the
sweep (see the closing note). Nothing was checked out; every read is `git show` / `git diff`
against refs.

### Grades

This slice uses the finer grades, because an absent symbol is a QUESTION, not a gap:

- **GAP (verified)** — symbol absent AND behaviour absent. Read for, not there under any name.
- **ADJUDICATE** — absent by name; the concept plainly exists on trunk; behaviour not read. **Not certified as a gap.**
- **NO GAP** — the behaviour landed, under this name or another. The something-else is cited.
- **PARK** — money path. Never self-approved.

Distribution over 169: **3 GAP (verified)** (one of them a money PARK) · **128 ADJUDICATE** · **38 NO GAP** (8 of those EMPTY).

### The docs-exclusion proof — it changed answers in this slice

Trunk-side search excludes `docs/` and every `*.md`. Measured on this slice by running the
index both ways:

- **125 of 169 branches** carry at least one symbol that exists on trunk ONLY in documentation.
- **440 distinct symbols** in this slice are docs-only on trunk.
- **4 branches flip bucket outright**: `keep-k8-ff850bb04`, `keep-red-cancelcheck`,
  `keep-step-dispatch-drain`, `keep-stepown-soc` all read HARVESTED/WEAK with `docs/` included
  and SURVIVOR with it excluded.
- Worked example, confirmed with an independent `git grep`: `KindAbiLaneGate` has **4** hits on
  trunk with docs, **0** without; `WIRE_PROTOCOL_WORDS` 2 → 0; `ALL_KINDS` 1 → 0. All three are
  `integration/rc-consolidated`'s `xtask/src/gates/kind_abi_lane.rs`, a gate file trunk does not have.
- Most-cited docs-only symbols: `a_row_this_reader_cannot_name_keeps_its_ordinal_rather_than_vanishing`
  (41 branches), `an_ordinal_declared_raise_key_is_recognised_as_ordinal` (41), `RAISE_SHAPE` (36),
  `KeyScope` (25), `_VIRTUAL_KEY_DIRECTORY` (23), `SinkRecordLegs` (21).

Adjudication then READS the docs for intent, per the corrected method: a symbol absent from code
because someone retired it on purpose is not a survivor.

### How the zeros were controlled

The whole method is greps, and a false zero reads exactly like a clean branch. Controls run:

- **Positive controls** (must be non-zero, and were): `build_app_from_config` 77 hits,
  `LoadedConfig` 7, `busbar_kernel` 5242, `Budget` 170, `resolve_model_context_max` 8 — all with
  `docs/` and `*.md` excluded.
- **Negative controls**: `ZzzNotARealSymbolQQ`, `definitely_absent_xyzzy` → 0 in both the
  in-memory index and `git grep`.
- **Index-vs-`git grep` agreement**: every claimed-absent symbol quoted in this slice was
  re-checked with an independent `git grep -w -F` against the pinned tree. `AppBuild` is the
  worked negative — my index says absent, `git grep` says absent, and the file `appbuild.rs`
  exists but defines no such symbol.
- **A real false zero was caught mid-sweep**: `git show "$T:crates/..."` in zsh parses `:c` as a
  history modifier and died with `unknown revision`; re-run as `git show "${T}:crates/..."`. The
  earlier form would have returned empty output that reads exactly like 'trunk does not have it'.
- Literal matching was tightened after a first pass: path-shaped literals (anything containing
  `/` or ending `.rs`/`.toml`/…) are DISCARDED, because paths do not survive the rename waves and
  scoring them produced ~1000 phantom absences on the `keep-land-*` branches alone. Shell-variable
  literals (`$failed_ids`, `$_st_groups`) were likewise discarded as noise.

### Cross-check against the second instrument

All 169 branches carry a prior verdict in `docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md`. **151 agree, 18 differ.**
Both directions are recorded below rather than silently resolved in my favour.

Of the 18: two I investigated and **the prior audit was right and I was wrong**
(`keep-admin-chain`, `keep-grammar-p2` — and `keep-gates-8`, three). Two I investigated and
**the prior audit was wrong** (`keep-kernel-p2`, graded `HARVESTED · symbol-complete` by the prior
audit and still carrying a real behavioural gap; `keep-auth-defects`, graded SURVIVOR but citing
only test-support symbols and missing the behavioural divergence).

### GAP (verified) and PARK — the actionable findings

| # | branch | grade | cell | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `origin/delete/keep-auth-defects` | GAP (verified) | `crates/busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs` (keys-arm branch) | **GAP (partial).** Three auth-chain defect fixes; two landed in BOTH trunk copies, one landed in only one. LANDED: `is_open()` = `chain.is_empty() && !keys_in_chain` (`busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs:380`, `busbar-kernel-identity/src/chain.rs:145`); cache-HIT no-re-put via `was_hit` (`chain.rs:199,220` and `auth/mod.rs:448,473`). NOT LANDED in the live copy: the keys arm flushing the buffered `pending_pass` rows. `busbar-kernel-identity/src/chain.rs:251-258` has the flush; `crates/busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs` — the copy `AppState.auth: Arc<crate::auth::AuthMiddleware>` (`state.rs:168`) actually runs — declares `pending_pass` at line 430 and then returns `keys_arm_verdict(...)` with no flush. Classic guard-in-the-other-copy. Effect: a `chain: [<cacheable module>, keys]` node re-runs every passing module on every request. Prior LEDGER said SURVIVOR/ADJUDICATED but cited only `RequestHeaders` / `from_chain_and_keys_for_test` (test support) — **it missed the behavioural divergence.** |
| 2 | `origin/delete/keep-kernel-p2` | GAP (verified) | `crates/busbar-kernel/src/pump.rs:197-203` | **GAP.** `xtask`-free kernel read. Branch's `Shape::OneShot` arm returns `Dispatch::Refuse{step:Decode, reason:InFlightCap}` when `start_one_shot()` fails; trunk `crates/busbar-kernel/src/pump.rs:197-203` still returns `Dispatch::Wait`. Trunk already HAS the `Dispatch::Refuse{step,reason}` variant (pump.rs:97-103), so this is a missed landing, not an unavailable seam. The pump keeps no queue, so a one-shot frame arriving at a spent permit is silently dropped — never rendered, counted or ended. The branch's other two commits are NO GAP: trunk `InFlight::insert` refuses a duplicate key via `shard.contains_key` BEFORE `claim_slot` (cleaner than the branch's claim-then-`fetch_sub`), and `take_and_release` is a behaviour-neutral extraction. **DISAGREES with prior LEDGER (`HARVESTED · symbol-complete`) — prior audit wrong on the pump.** |
| 3 | `origin/delete/keep-llm-abort` | PARK (money) | `crates/busbar-kernel/src/teller.rs:1156-1174` | **PARK (money) + NO GAP on the rate half.** NO GAP: the one-decimal-to-money consolidation landed — `busbar-kernel-ledger/src/cost/rate.rs:40::nano_rate` is the single conversion and `busbar-kernel-budget/src/price.rs:82-85` calls it instead of carrying a copy; the ten-thousand-rate agreement test survives at `crates/busbar-kernel-ledger/tests/rate_conversion_agreement.rs` whose header reads 'There used to be two.' PARK: the abandoned-unit settle seam did NOT land. Trunk `crates/busbar-kernel/src/teller.rs:1156-1174` `impl Drop for Abandoned` still runs `let _ended = terminal(...)` and DISCARDS the end, keeping the very comment the branch replaced. There is no `Units::abandoned` seam on trunk. On the normal path the `Ended::Settled{requests, fee, end}` that `terminal` returns is what `crates/busbar/src/root/harness.rs:140` and `crates/busbar/src/root/units_llm.rs:412-417` read to settle onto the plane's own book/journal; on every `Abort::Kernel{reason: ClientGone}` that settle never runs. Also absent: the `charged: bool` argument + `debug_assert!(!charged)` on `audit_refused` (trunk `crates/busbar-llm/src/unit/audit.rs:406` takes three args). |

priced-at-admission and the back-dating question) which trunk does NOT have; trunk has the
adjacent `docs/design/rate-card-history.md`. Money-adjacent design prose, not code.

### `vk` batch 4 — what the SYMBOL pass could not see

Per the correction, a second instrument was run over all 172: every `+`/`-` line inside a
NON-test source file on an auth/money/kernel/egress/secret path that contains an operator, a
comparison, a clamp or a guard, keeping only lines DELETED without an identical line added back —
i.e. changed behaviour, with or without a new symbol. 172 branches scanned; **44 have a surgical
set of 1-6 such lines**, which is where a silent delta hides. Every one of those 44 was read.

Two of the three findings below add **no new symbol at all** and are invisible to every symbol
method, including the prior audit's.

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/verify/keep-queue-drain` (and the chain `queue-repick-1 ⊂ 2 ⊂ 3 ⊂ 4 ⊂ 5 ⊂ queue-drain`; same delta on `keep-plugin-abi-fix`) | SURVIVOR | — | — | `read_file_secret` · `MAX_FILE_SECRET_BYTES` · `VarError::NotUnicode` arm · `!v.trim().is_empty()` · `bytes.iter().all(u8::is_ascii_whitespace)` | **GAP (verified) — three residual guards on the secret resolver, one NO GAP.** Adjudicated guard by guard against trunk `crates/api/src/secret.rs`: **(a) size cap — NO GAP, it landed renamed.** Branch `read_file_secret`/`MAX_FILE_SECRET_BYTES` is trunk `read_secret_file_bounded`/`MAX_SECRET_FILE_BYTES` (`secret.rs:188-204`), same read-one-past-the-cap trick, so the buffer is bounded and `/dev/zero` cannot exhaust memory. **(b) BLANK IS NOT EMPTY on trunk — GAP.** `secret.rs:147` accepts `Ok(v) if !v.is_empty()`, so an env secret of `"   "` — what a templating step that produced nothing, an indented here-doc, or a placeholder mount leaves behind — boots as a valid credential and fails at the first upstream call instead of at boot. Trunk trims the reference NAME (`secret.rs:213`) and the PATH (`:228`) but **not the value**; the branch closes that asymmetry, and does it on the BYTES for the `file:` arm so a binary key is never judged as text. **(c) `VarError::NotUnicode` reported as "unset" — GAP.** `secret.rs:152` is `Err(_) => "environment variable '{var}' is unset"`, so a variable that IS set but arrived mis-encoded sends the operator to set a variable that is already set. `NotUnicode` is **0 trunk code files, 1 doc file** — docs-only, the trap again. **(d) non-regular file accepted — GAP (low).** Branch refuses a device/pipe/directory via `metadata().is_file()` (`metadata`, not `symlink_metadata`, because a Kubernetes secret mount IS a symlink); `is_file` appears **0 times in trunk's `crates/api/src/secret.rs`**, so a FIFO at a `file:` secret path blocks the boot read rather than being refused. Bounded by (a), so a hang and not an exhaustion. **Recommendation:** take (b), (c), (d); (a) is already there. |
| `origin/verify/keep-tests-plugins` — second finding on the same branch | SURVIVOR | — | — | `assert_descriptive` in `crates/plugin-testkit/src/lib.rs`; 27 per-crate `*_conformance.rs` on `keep-k8-a1f2c00bc` | **PARTLY a deliberate retirement, partly a real gap — and the two must not be confused.** `plugin-testkit` was **deliberately deleted by owner ruling 2026-09-22** (`1.6.0-LEDGER.md:935`, BR-B5; #2/#31 forbid a shared test util between plugins), so `assert_descriptive` and the branch's `crates/plugin-testkit/src/{transport,unit}_conformance.rs` are **NO GAP — do not port them.** But the RE-INLINING is incomplete, and this slice measures it independently: trunk has **0** files matching `(auth\|transport\|unit\|secret\|hook)_conformance`, while `origin/verify/keep-k8-a1f2c00bc` (tip of `fa12a3c45 ⊂ c8ab54376 ⊂ e0ed53761 ⊂ 569d3d77c ⊂ 990458683 ⊂ a1f2c00bc`, itself descended from `k9-seam-exittoken-confinement`) carries **27 suites in exactly the per-crate `crates/<crate>/tests/<kind>_conformance.rs` form the ruling requires**: auth 2, hook 2, secret 2, transport 7, unit 14. Concurs with BR-B5 from a different slice; adds the single branch that carries all 27. |

**And one finding I did NOT make, recorded because the discipline is the point.** Twenty branches
delete `if let (Some(r), Some(cred)) = (revocations, candidate)` from the auth chain and replace the
separately-bound `KeyVerifier` + `RevocationView` pair with one `VirtualKeyDirectory` face, *"so a
deployment cannot end up with a verifier and a denylist that disagree."* Trunk still has the
two-`Option` shape at `crates/busbar-kernel-identity/src/chain.rs:291` and `unit.rs:110`, which reads
exactly like a fail-open: identify the principal with `Some(keys)`, skip the denylist with
`None` revocations. **It is not reachable.** `crates/busbar/src/root/auth_bindings.rs:168-176` holds
ONE `directory: Option<DirectoryArm>` and projects BOTH `keys()` and `revocations()` from it, so they
are `Some`/`Some` or `None`/`None` and never split; `without_directory()` documents the `None` case
as fail-closed. `VirtualKeyDirectory` is on trunk in 7 code files. **NO GAP** — the face landed, the
residual `Option` pair is defence-in-depth, and reporting it would have been a false positive on the
auth path. Reading the BINDING rather than the signature is what prevented it.

Same check, same outcome, on `keep-api-kinds-to-contract`'s deletion of `constant_time_eq` from
`crates/api/src/auth.rs`: a **relocation**, not a removal — trunk has it at
`crates/busbar-contract/src/redacted.rs:124`. **NO GAP.** (Noted in passing, outside this sweep's
scope: trunk carries a *second* copy at `crates/busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs:636`. Two spellings of
one constant-time primitive is the shape that drifts.)

`codeaudit-fix/*` (20) · `wip/*` (6) · `abi-fix/*` (5) · `gate-fix/*` (3) · `plane-fold/*` (2) ·
`integration/*` (2) · `fix/*` (2) · `badge/ ci/ deps/ gate/ infra/ perf/ reference/ scratch/ spec/ test/` (1 each).
Every diff read, none sampled. Trunk pinned `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`;
**re-verified at `25650661afd7c8c73c3da60410d1fbf6893e69f3`** after trunk moved mid-sweep —
all eleven GAP rows below still hold at the newer head.

Grades: **GAP (verified)** symbol AND behaviour absent, read for · **ADJUDICATE** absent by name,
concept present, behaviour not read · **NO GAP** landed, here or under another name (cited) ·
**PARK** money, owner-only.

### GAP (verified) — 11 rows

| # | branch | symbol / behaviour | branch-side path | trunk evidence | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A6-1 | `codeaudit-fix/transport-http-r2` | `the_scan_would_catch_a_planted_name` asserts `contains_word(…) \|\| "A2A_MOUNT".contains("A2A")` — the right operand is literal-on-literal, **always true**, so the self-test of the no-plane-names scanner cannot fail even if `contains_word` stops firing on the `_` boundary | `crates/busbar-transport-http/tests/no_plane_names.rs:192` | tautology **verbatim on trunk at 2 sites**: `busbar-transport-{grpc,http}/tests/no_plane_names.rs:192` | **GAP** — a blind self-test. Prior `1.6.0-LEDGER.md` graded this branch HARVESTED, citing only the MED (smuggling) half; the LOW was never checked. **Prior audit wrong.** |
| A6-2 | `codeaudit-fix/transport-sse-r2` | (a) `SSE_FIELDS` holds names **with** colons and is matched by `line.starts_with` — a legal frame whose only line is a bare `data` (no colon, empty value per the event-stream grammar) is dropped as a keepalive comment, and a `datastream:` line that is *not* the `data` field is admitted; (b) `event_type = rest.trim()` eats a second leading space and all trailing whitespace where the grammar strips exactly one leading U+0020 — and the `data:` arm one line below already does it right | `crates/busbar-transport-sse/src/proto.rs` | both live on trunk: `proto.rs:110` `const SSE_FIELDS: [&[u8]; 4] = [b"data:", b"event:", b"id:", b"retry:"]`, `:128` `.starts_with(field)`, `:153` `rest.trim().to_string()` | **GAP** ×2 (LOW). (b) is the no-new-symbol class — a `.trim()` that should be `.strip_prefix(' ')`. Prior ledger: HARVESTED, "1 absent string, a fixture" — it graded the r1 HIGH, not the r2 LOWs. **Prior audit wrong.** |
| A6-3 | `codeaudit-fix/transport-tls-r2` | rename `READ_CHUNK_BYTES` → `TLS_READ_CHUNK_BYTES`; three sibling transports each export a `pub const READ_CHUNK_BYTES` under one name carrying two meanings | `crates/busbar-transport-tls/src/lib.rs:63` | `TLS_READ_CHUNK_BYTES` absent. Trunk keeps all three: `transport-tcp/src/lib.rs:50` `= 16*1024`, `transport-tls/src/lib.rs:55` `= 16*1024`, `transport-http/src/lib.rs:103` `= busbar_contract::MAX_CURSOR_BYTES` — **different value AND different meaning** (header-prefix scan cap) | **GAP** (LOW) — silent wrong-binding hazard for a consumer reaching for `..::READ_CHUNK_BYTES`. The MED half (`UnexpectedEof => Reset`) **did** land (`lib.rs:394`). Prior ledger HARVESTED cited only the MED. |
| A6-4 | `codeaudit-fix/transport-ws-r2` | `frame_meta_honesty_catches_inflating_and_deflating_fixtures` — a must-turn-red cell that perturbs a real emitted frame ±1 byte. `upgrade_then_round_trip_byte_exact` asserts `frame.meta.bytes == payload.len()` against the one correct value the transport produced: green over exactly one number, can never go red | `crates/busbar-transport-ws/src/tests/battery.rs` | the exact cell exists **only** in `transport-sse/src/tests/mod.rs:630` and `transport-tcp/src/tests/mod.rs:462`. **ws, tls, stdio, grpc have none.** Trunk ws still asserts the single value at `battery.rs:61` | **GAP** (MED, instrument). Money-adjacent, **not** certified money: `FrameMeta.bytes` is documented as the wire byte count, and at trunk `.meta.bytes` has **0 readers outside the transport crates** — so it is not on the billed path *today*. ### PARK — money (binary; owner rules, never self-approved)

**A6-P1 · cell: the fractional token count on the LLM usage read path · branch `integration/reland`**

Two integration lineages ruled **oppositely** on the same wire shape, and only one is on trunk.

* branch `crates/busbar-llm-codec/src/usage_tail.rs::token_count` — a fractional value **rounds UP**
  (`11.1 → 12`, `0.001 → 1`), test `fractional_double_rounds_up`, rationale *"a partially-consumed
  billing unit is still a whole unit charged, and this must never bill LESS than the provider reported."*
* trunk `crates/busbar-llm-codec/src/usage_count.rs::read_count_u64` — a fractional value is
  **REJECTED** (`None`), rationale *"rejected, never rounded: rounding would invent a quantity the
  provider never reported."*

Both are defensible; they cannot both be right, and no row records which was ruled.

Two further facts measured on trunk, in the same read:

1. `UnreadableCount` — the type whose whole purpose is to keep *"the provider reported nothing"*
   apart from *"the provider reported something this build does not understand"* — is constructed
   only inside `usage_count.rs:123` and **named by no other file in the workspace**. Its designated
   caller `billed_count` (`usage_count.rs:116`, *"ABSENT IS ZERO, UNREADABLE IS A REFUSAL … #42:
   money-sacred, never a silent 0"*) therefore reaches no reader.
2. Two sites bypass `billed_count` with exactly the idiom its own doc names as the defect
   (*"Why this exists rather than `.and_then(read_count_u64).unwrap_or(0)`"*):
   `crates/busbar-llm-codec/src/gemini/mod.rs:378` and
   `crates/busbar-llm-codec/src/openai_chat/reader.rs:920`. At those two sites a present-but-unreadable
   count becomes a billed **zero** — the failure the module was written to prevent.

**Root cause** — the fractional rule was decided twice, on two lineages, and the reconciliation never
happened; the refusal type landed without its callers.
**Recommendation** — owner rules fractional (reject vs round-up) and records it; then either route the
two bypass sites through `billed_count` or record why each is exempt. **Not self-approved. No byte
of this touched.**

### Docs-exclusion proof — the exclusion changed an answer

6,318 identifiers exist on trunk **only** in `docs/`. Bucketing with and without `docs/` in the
trunk-side search flipped **`abi-fix/version-constants`** from `HARVESTED` to `SURVIVOR`, on one
symbol: **`TRANSPORT_KIND_ABI`** — `git grep -F 'TRANSPORT_KIND_ABI' <trunk> -- ':(exclude)docs/'`
returns **rc=1, zero hits**, while the docs search returns exactly one line:
`docs/design/1.6.0-LEDGER.md:967`, the prior audit's own row about this branch.
A docs-including tool reads the audit's prose as the code it is about.

Then **docs read for intent**, which cut the other way and is the reason A6-6 and A6-8 are GAPs
while `gate-fix/kind-isolation-vocab` is not: `kind-abi-lane` is absent from code but **locked** in
DECISIONS #30 (`BUSBAR-1.6.0.md:354`) with no retirement anywhere → GAP. `WIRE_PROTOCOL_WORDS` is
absent from code and **explicitly HELD pending owner ratify** in the decisions queue → not a gap.
Same signal, opposite verdicts, and only the docs say which.
`abi-fix/version-constants` itself is NO GAP for the same reason: trunk deliberately keeps
`TRANSPORT_ABI`/`STORE_ABI`, and the prior ledger row says so.

on the strength of the **HIGH/MED** half, while the **LOW** half of the same commit is still on

### How the zeros were controlled

Every "absent" is an `rc=1` from a `git grep` whose **identical shape** returns `rc=0` on a
known-present control in the same command (`hot_path_perf` present vs `kind_abi_lane` absent;
`READ_CHUNK_BYTES` present vs `TLS_READ_CHUNK_BYTES` absent). Four false zeros were caught and
corrected **in this sweep**, each by its own control:

1. `git ls-tree -- 'crates/busbar-transport-*/tests/conformance.rs'` → **0**. `ls-tree` does not
   glob and rejects `:(glob)`; the control `…/src/meta.rs` returned 0 where 7 exist. Redone as
   `ls-tree -r --name-only | grep -E` → 7 controls, 0 conformance files. A6-5 survived the redo.
2. `"$T:qa/construction.toml"` → zsh ate `:q` as a history modifier and git reported
   `…172a/construction.toml: unknown revision`. Every later path read used `"$T":"<path>"`.
3. `grep -nE '^\s+fn '` over a file known to contain them → **0**. macOS POSIX ERE has no `\s`.
   Re-run with `[[:space:]]`.
4. `git grep … | head -N; echo rc=$?` → reported the rc of `head`, not `grep`. Every rc check was
   redone unpiped, writing to a file and counting lines.

Corpus-level guards: the trunk corpus builder asserts >1,000 code files, >1 MB code text and
>100 KB docs text before any bucketing (measured: 3,513 files / 50.3 MB / 7.4 MB, 72,848 code
identifiers vs 28,104 docs identifiers); the branch enumerator asserts >800 remote refs (819).
Trunk moved from `d2548391b` to `25650661a` mid-sweep; **all eleven GAP rows were re-verified at
the newer head** and all eleven still hold — the one apparent change
(`frame_meta_honesty_…` reading present) resolved to its **sse and tcp** siblings, not ws, so A6-4 stands.
### Closing note — trunk moved under this slice, and the findings were re-checked

The slice was indexed against `d2548391bc3a76b4a01c3dfa4c486efebe93a172`. Trunk advanced to
`25650661afd7c8c73c3da60410d1fbf6893e69f3` while the sweep ran (14 files: the config prepass,
secret-hygiene, `no_deferral` and the sweep ledger itself — none of them in the cells below).

All three `GAP (verified)` findings were re-read at the later SHA and **all three still hold**:

- `crates/busbar-kernel/src/pump.rs:197-203` — `Shape::OneShot` still answers `Dispatch::Wait`.
- `crates/busbar-kernel/src/auth/mod.rs:504-506` — the keys arm still returns without flushing
  `pending_pass`, while `busbar-kernel-identity/src/chain.rs:251-258` still does flush it.
- `crates/busbar-kernel/src/teller.rs:1156-1159` — `Abandoned::drop` still discards the sealed end.

The docs-exclusion proof also re-verified at the later SHA: `KindAbiLaneGate` 4→0,
`WIRE_PROTOCOL_WORDS` 2→0, `ALL_KINDS` 1→0 hits when `docs/` and `*.md` are excluded.

**Confidence.** The 3 `GAP` rows and the `NO GAP` rows that cite a trunk file and line were read
by hand. The 128 `ADJUDICATE` rows were NOT behaviour-read — they are absent-by-name only and
must not be reported as gaps. The largest of them (`keep-llm-parse-to-plane`, `keep-mount-chain-*`,
`keep-a2a-on-mount`, `keep-k8-6e577bf42`, each 375-494 absent symbols) are whole superseded
architectures — `busbar-caps`, `busbar-contract-transport`, `busbar-core`, `busbar-substrate`
are crates trunk deleted — and should be adjudicated as design decisions, not triaged symbol by
symbol.

### `vk` batch 5 — the money copies, adjudicated one at a time

The behaviour scan put four branches on `crates/busbar-unit-admission/src/price.rs`, the budget
DOOR. That file is where a request is JUDGED, while `busbar-*-ledger/cost/` is where it is BILLED,
so any divergence between them is a request judged at one arithmetic and billed at another —
the failure trunk's own commit `8f083cc38` (*"the guard was in the other copy"*) closed today for
the SUM. Each copy was compared line by line rather than by name.

| branch | bucket | symbols-in | symbols-found | absent symbols | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `origin/verify/keep-plane-llm-p2` | SURVIVOR by name | — | — | the branch's inline `fn nanos(utok: f64)` (deleted), `busbar_unit_cost::nano_rate` | **NO GAP — landed verbatim, crate rename applied.** The branch deletes the door's private second copy of the rate projection and routes all four fields through the pricing law. Trunk `crates/busbar-kernel-budget/src/price.rs:75-87` carries **the same change and the same doc paragraph**, calling `busbar_kernel_ledger::cost::nano_rate` four times.
